### A53. OneSpace · `docs/R40_UX_CONSISTENCY_AUDIT.md`

*First written 14 Nov 2026 · last changed 14 Nov 2026 · Status: Historical record (iteration notes / test scripts / setup at that date).*

#### R40 — OneSpace UX consistency audit and staged implementation
Date: 2026-11-14. A source-level first pass, **not** a claim that every
screen and field has been fully standardized or phone-tested.

##### Universal product contract
1. **Collection screens:** AppBar top-right has History (archived and
   recoverable items) when records can be archived, and Filters when
   state, owner, privacy, category or other filters apply. Search should
   be accessible near the top of a list, with clear reset state.
   Historical logs are distinct from archived content.
2. **Record actions:** Provide Edit, Archive/Remove, Cancel, Reopen,
   Restore or Detach only where meaningful and authorized. No hard
   delete for records where referential integrity, historic receipts or
   partner outcomes require preservation. Destructive actions require
   context and confirmation. View-only recipients do not get edit actions.
3. **Who/what/when:** Server-authenticated actor, fields affected and
   timestamp. No password, health details, private text or media copies
   in general audit log; preserve existing feature-specific revisions.
   History is append-only and follows the **original object RLS**.
4. **Mentions:** `@partner` for authorized, editable *shared free-text*
   fields and rich text. Date, currency, rating, visibility, location,
   checkboxes, secret/private or disabled fields should not offer a
   mention control. Server must own authorization, notification and
   deduplication; text insertion alone is **not** working notification.
5. **Search:** Global discovery plus local content/metadata search for
   every data-bearing module; RLS mandatory. Not every multimedia
   attachment should have its OCR/transcription indexed automatically.
6. **Contrast:** At least WCAG 4.5:1 ordinary text and 3:1 large text
   and UI icons, in four palettes, system/light/dark, and seasonal themes.
   Visual overrides including translucent image overlays need separate
   on-device checks for actual composited backgrounds.

##### Implementation delivered this batch
- Reusable `RecordListTopActions` with top-right History and Filters.
- Goals: top-right archived History + status Filters, title/description search.
- Memories: top-right archived History + visibility Filters;
  existing people/place/photo-tag search retained.
- Notes: top-right archived History + visibility Filters;
  existing rich-text content search retained.
- Reminders: top-right archived History + completed Filters,
  title/details search, existing recipient outcome history retained.
- Global Search: index Goals and Memories in addition to previous
  categories; top-right result-type filter and direct goal/memory/task
  record deep links. Search still does not cover every nested record.
- Theme: contrast test iterates 4 palettes × 2 brightness modes × all
  seasonal treatments for core text surfaces and semantic containers.
- Additive migration 0103: server-only actor/field/time history for
  notes, tasks, goals, memories, calendar events, reminders, occasions,
  shared experiences. Does not backfill old edits. RLS test 0024 PASSED,
  including private partner isolation, archival and restoration.
- Shared history view linked from 8 record UI surfaces.
- Rich-text Quill Notes now offer @partner insertion in editable shared
  documents; existing note mention trigger handles actual alert delivery.

##### Source-level audit findings / remaining gaps
| Module | Existing coverage | Remaining work |
|---|---|---|
| Today / Dashboard | Aggregated relationship indicators | Audit every CTA, theme overlay and accessible contrast |
| Coordinate / Activities | Activity filters, text search, history & per-component changes | Normalize top placement and component-level action permissions |
| To-do / Checklists | Top filters, status history, editable lists, R34 crash fix, R37 speech | Per-subtask action/mention rules, private checkmark audit |
| Notes | Rich Delta, images, R38/R39 linked records; now top toolbar | Rich mentions phone QA, attachment/field-level audit |
| Goals | CRUD-style editor, archive/restore; now top toolbar and search | Confirm cancel/status semantics and progress history |
| Memories / Scrapbooks | Search across text/photo metadata, archive/restore; now top toolbar | Photo and layout-level changes, image text contrast |
| Calendar | History at top, events, mentions, source notes | Standardize filters, recurring edit and timezone validation |
| Reminders | Per-recipient outcome history; now top toolbar and text search | Source-level archive notifications and fine-grained history |
| Contacts | Top history + search + restore | Align filters with common toolbar, validate each card action |
| Occasions | Private/shared planning, shopping/budget/revisions | Full module-level search, per-step actions/history consistency |
| Shared Experiences | Suggestions, interest, comments, reviews, revisions | Full comment/review search and deletion/restore model |
| Trips / Documents | Items, documents, trip preparation | Search all attachment metadata, offline availability, restore flows |
| Check-ins / Letters / Chat | Dedicated interaction workflows | Mentions and message editing may have intentionally different lifecycle rules |
| Notifications | Read/unread controls | Top filter consistency, immutable notification event history |
| Settings / Profile / Auth | Settings and account controls | Controls are not general CRUD records; audit security changes separately |
| Global Search | RLS title search across 14 categories | Nested object search, body/metadata indexing, archived opt-in |

##### Safety, action-policy nuances
- Users may **archive / restore** sync records. Hard deletion is not
  automatic because connected reminders, child records or audit history
  might become orphaned. The record remains accessible according to RLS.
- A private note shared read-only is still not editable by its recipient.
- A shared object must never link or reveal a private surprise.
- Never generate notifications from mere rendering/search/edit preview.
- No automatic @mention triggered for private content.
- A physical-device crash fix is not established by source tests alone.

##### Next iteration priorities
1. Green Flutter/analyzer/test and signed Android preview for current R40.
2. Expand top History/Filters to all remaining collection screens.
3. Complete per-component CRUD/restore permission matrix, including
   dependent objects, comments and attachments.
4. Add authorized mentions and notification routing for remaining
   *supported free-text* fields, with backend integration tests.
5. Search nested metadata/child items and authorized archived records.
6. On-device contrast and font scaling testing for image-based layouts,
   empty states, dark mode and season overlays.

##### Acceptance
- Both existing phone accounts upgraded in place without data reset.
- History and Filters always accessible at screen top for applicable
  collections; audit logs record authenticated who/what/when.
- Search respects private/shared RLS.
- Shared Quill @mention notifies only intended partner; private note
  cannot notify.
- Confirm checkout of R34 checklist expansion, R37 speech, R35–R36
  reminders, R38 Notes and R39 backlinks, and FCM gestures.

##### Palette contrast clean-up
- Removed hard-coded `Colors.red` error text from Goals, Memories and Notes in favor of `Theme.of(context).colorScheme.error`.
- Automated 4-palette × 2-mode × seasonal scheme regression remains required; physical composited-image contrast remains separate QA.

##### Subsequent R41–R42 source work (separate from signed R40)
- R41: shared Reminders now have a real @partner insertion and server notification trigger 0104; SQL 0025 recipient-side RLS test PASSED. Notifications All/Unread moved to top-right, and task notifications navigate to the exact checklist.
- R42: Global Search additionally queries text content in Activities, Calendar details, Notes body, Goals description, Memories body, Reminders details and Shared Experience description. Results are deduplicated by type and record ID, queried only through source table RLS, without copying private data into a search index. This still does not include every nested component or archived record.
- Both require Flutter analyzer, tests, signed APK and two-device acceptance before feature completion claims.

*First written 14 Nov 2026 · last changed 14 Nov 2026 · Status: Historical record (iteration notes / test scripts / setup at that date).*

#### R41 — Authorized reminders mentions and notifications UX

##### Delivered
- Shared Reminder text details now offers a real @partner insertion
  control, using the authenticated partner display name.
- Additive SQL migration 0104 installed the actual server notification
  trigger. A mention only notifies when the reminder is shared, active
  and the text contains the other partner's full @display_name.
- Does not notify when the reminder is private; does not expose private
  reminder details to the other account.
- Server-owned (recipient,kind,object_id) conflict key deduplicates
  mention alerts; editing unrelated data does not create new mentions.
- Notification deep link opens the precise Reminder record, and task
  notifications open the precise checklist, not just its module.
- Notification list filter moved to top-right. Mark-all-read remains
  accessible and does not erase notification history.
- General R40 record action history remains independent from recipient
  reminder outcome history and mention alerts.

##### Security evidence
- R41 two-partner rollback-only SQL test 0025 PASS: authenticated
  sender created shared mention, other partner account could read
  the mention alert, private reminder emitted no mention, private
  reminder remained invisible, and unrelated edit did not duplicate
  mention. All test data rolled back.
- Source RLS and user preferences (quiet hours, pairing, push tokens)
  are unchanged by the notification trigger.
- No token, password, document body or private note text is copied
  into the notification headline.

##### Remaining mention roadmap
- Goal and Memory free-text fields still need corresponding object
  kinds, secure notifications and exact-object routing. Do NOT present
  text insertion as full mention functionality before server tests.
- Other components' rich content, comments and attachments require
  their own lifecycle and revocation logic. No mention controls on
  dates, currency, selects, status fields or private text.
- Full automated Flutter verification and signed preview required;
  R40 Release 415 was pinned earlier and does not include this batch.

##### Phone QA
1. On phone A create a shared Reminder and @mention partner in details.
2. On phone B verify mention alert opens exact reminder and normal
   reminder schedule/recipient status remains independent.
3. Create a private reminder containing identical text; phone B must
   receive no mention and see no private reminder.
4. Change a shared reminder without changing mention text: no duplicate.
5. Verify Notifications top filter All/Unread, Mark all read,
   checklist deep-link and R34 expanded checklist remain functional.
6. Verify the R40 dark/light season contrast and audit history UI.

### A55. OneSpace · `docs/R43_COLLECTION_HISTORY_FILTERS_SEARCH_QA.md`

*First written 14 Nov 2026 · last changed 14 Nov 2026 · Status: Historical record (iteration notes / test scripts / setup at that date).*

#### R43 — Collection-screen History, Filters and searchable content

##### Scope built
- **Trips & journeys:** standardized top-right History/Filters using
  RecordListTopActions; archived-only history remains recoverable;
  Upcoming/Past/All filters moved into the top sheet; local search now
  checks title, destination, origin and trip notes.
- **Occasions & surprises:** top-right History/Filters; Upcoming,
  Only me, Both of us and All filters; on-screen search checks title,
  honoree, notes and occasion kind, all already RLS-filtered.
- **People & Contacts:** top-right Recently Deleted History and
  All/Saved directly/From Activities filters; existing contact-edit
  history is kept as a separate top-right action. Search of contact
  card fields is unchanged. Restore does not delete activity data.
- **Shared Experiences:** top-right archived History and centralized
  Status/Category/Mutual filters. Existing revision history remains
  separate and accessible. Search now also includes description.
  Discovery category tiles are retained as deliberate shortcuts.
- **Coordinate (embedded tab):** History and Filters sit in its own
  top header (no nested app bar). Archived rows are shown only through
  History, not mixed into the All active filter; existing activity
  title/body search and separate Activity revision history remain.
- **Daily Check-ins:** top-right conversation History, conversation
  status and journal visibility Filters. Two independent searchable
  surfaces cover authorized check-in messages/gratitude and partner
  conversation messages/topics, without merging private content.

##### UX contract
- Archived/deleted records and **edit/action history** are not the
  same thing. Both are available where applicable.
- History refers to recoverable, archived records. Filter sheets
  adjust status/visibility/category. Search stays at the top of the
  screen and must work when a filter or History view is selected.
- View-only and private content protections remain on the existing
  backend and database policies; presentation controls never confer
  rights. No new Supabase grants and no user data migrations in R43.
- Restore and edit actions remain the original ones, rather than
  introducing unsafe hard-delete shortcuts.
- Avoid extra backend queries when toggling local collection filters
  or searching loaded rows. Fetch is still limited by each screen's
  original RLS and pagination; global search provides wider coverage.

##### Tests and release gate
- Run all committed Flutter dependencies, analyzer and tests,
  especially legacy/new checklist expansion, native voice, note links,
  reminder mentions, top-bar widget tests and theme contrast.
- No change to package name, pairing, token registration, Firebase
  configuration, privacy or signing keys.
- Signed Android APK must independently repeat the gate, compile
  Kotlin and be installed in place on both phones.
- The previous R42 Release 416 is a separately pinned version and
  does not contain R43. Never claim new features are in 416.

##### Acceptance on two Android phones
1. Open Trips/Occasions/Contacts/Experiences/Coordinate/Check-ins.
   History and Filters appear in each screen's top action area.
2. Switch History on/off and confirm archived content isn't mixed
   into normal lists. Restore an authorized archived item.
3. Check filter combinations and search within title, notes, names,
   destinations, experience descriptions, gratitude and conversation
   messages; confirm no cross-partner private result.
4. Confirm existing revision/event history remains independent of
   archived records and shows who/what/when where supported.
5. Recheck R34 legacy/new checklist expansion without crashes,
   R37 voice reading, note references, occasion prep and FCM.
6. Manually inspect font/background contrast across palette/mode/
   holiday combinations, dynamic text scaling and image overlays.

##### Remaining work
- Other screens: Goals/Memories/Notes/Reminders have R40 controls,
  but individual editors, nested components, comments, photo galleries
  and attachments still need a field-level CRUD/restore audit.
- Authorized @mentions and action-history coverage are still not
  universal; checkin confidentiality takes precedence over mentions.
- Full nested/attachment search and safely searchable archived
  content are future R44+ work. Global Search still uses capped
  original table queries, not a universal full-text index.

### A56. OneSpace · `docs/R44_EXPANDED_RECORD_ACTION_AUDIT_QA.md`

*First written 14 Nov 2026 · last changed 14 Nov 2026 · Status: Historical record (iteration notes / test scripts / setup at that date).*

#### R44 — Expanded who / what / when audit history

##### Purpose
Continue R40 action-audit standard for additional relationship data:
- Trips & journeys (`shared_trips`)
- Standalone saved contacts (`saved_contacts`)
- Personal daily check-ins (`daily_checkins`)
- Partner check-in requests/responses (`partner_checkin_requests`)

##### Backend
- Additive migration 0105 extends the existing
  `record_action_history` kinds and its **source-object RLS**.
- Private trips, contacts and check-in histories are visible only to
  accounts already authorized to read their source record.
- A new trigger function writes server-observed actor `auth.uid()`,
  event time, action and names of changed fields. It does not copy
  names, email, phone numbers, emotions, message text, credentials,
  media, or travel-document contents into the general audit log.
- For trips, status cancellation/reopening and archive/restore are
  differentiated. Saved contact archive and restore are recorded.
- Other changes are labeled edited; creation is labeled created.
- Existing individual feature revision histories remain available.
  No retroactive reconstruction of old history is attempted.
- Historical events are immutable through app Data API grants.
- No new client write privileges, and no existing user data deleted.

##### Verification
- SQL rollback-only test 0026 PASSED after migration 0105:
  test fixtures as migration owner because direct table insert is
  restricted (normal app writes may use RPC).
  **History reads** are tested under authenticated partner A/B RLS.
- Four new event types generated, trip cancellation recorded,
  contact archive/restore recorded, private gratitude edit logged as
  the *field name* only, and partner B could see shared trip actions
  but not private contact/check-in actions.
- No test fixtures persisted.
- New action history UI uses the existing RecordActionHistoryButton/
  View, which fetches through table RLS.
- Flutter analyzer and full tests required before signed Android APK.

##### UI entry points
- Trip detail app bar: server Record action history; existing trip
  revision history remains distinct.
- Expanded saved contact: both prior Contact edit history and new
  server Record action history for direct saved contacts only.
- Partner check-in request expansion: server action history.
- Daily Check-in expansion: server action history.

##### Important boundaries / next gaps
- Child records such as trip documents, experience reviews,
  calendar subcomponents, and per-user checklist checkmarks need
  separate sensitive-data retention policies before standardized
  history can be added.
- Entries with no server-created events before this migration do not
  have historical backfill.
- Some records use dedicated revision logs that show detailed
  before/after fields; the generic audit is metadata-only.
- If source access is revoked, the history becomes unreadable by
  that account even if the event once existed.

##### Two-phone acceptance
1. Update in-place with no data loss.
2. Edit shared Trip; both accounts see actor and changed fields.
3. Archive/restore saved private contact; only its owner sees events.
4. Create/edit a private daily check-in; the other phone must never
   show its event history, notes or notification.
5. Send a partner check-in request and respond; both entitled users
   may see request-history metadata, with original lifecycle intact.
6. Reconfirm shared/private Notes, reminders/mentions, checklist
   expansions, speech, calendar, occasions and push notifications.

### A57. OneSpace · `docs/R45_EXPERIENCE_COMMENT_LIFECYCLE_QA.md`

*First written 14 Nov 2026 · last changed 14 Nov 2026 · Status: Historical record (iteration notes / test scripts / setup at that date).*

#### R45 — Shared Experience conversation lifecycle and permission gates

##### Implemented
- Author can edit, retract and restore individual comments; partner can read
  active comments but cannot edit them or see retracted text, even from a direct
  Data API query. Author can opt to show *their own* retracted comments.
- Each comment has an immutable creator, server-updated concurrency timestamp,
  and server-recorded created/edited/retracted/restored events with actor,
  changed field names and time. **No comment body** enters the audit log.
- Conversation has local multiword search across comments, including author's
  own retracted comments only when opted in.
- New comments can insert an @partner mention; the existing comment-send
  notification remains responsible for delivery. Editing does not send alerts.
- No hard-delete, no removal of legacy tasks/checklists, and no data reset.

##### Deployment order
1. Apply migration 0106 before installing new APK. This adds a new nullable-
   compatibility timestamp (with default), one column update grant,
   author/privacy-safe SELECT policy and a comment-scoped audit kind.
2. Run rollback-only SQL test 0027 as *authenticated* partner and author;
   ensure visible active, invisible retracted, unauthorized update rejected,
   history/restore safe and no personal comment bodies in audit.
3. Run Flutter analyzer and full tests, including checklist legacy expansion.
4. Build one signed preview APK only after green CI. Install on both existing
   phones without signing, package or account data changes.

##### Remaining product-wide consistency work
- Attachment/photo and nested component edit/restore coverage.
- More granular search across child items, archived opt-in and permission
  boundaries, plus additional module text mentions with server routing.
- Manual screenshot contrast checks across all palettes/light-dark and
  image overlays, and both-device notification tap/recipient checks.

### A58. OneSpace · `docs/DECISION_LOG.md`

*First written 15 Nov 2026 · last changed 20 Nov 2026 · Status: Active, continued in §4 (D81+). Overridden items are marked ⚠️ inline.*

#### OneSpace — Decision Log and Design Rules

This file is the single source of truth for product decisions made with Ahmed (owner) since 15 Nov 2026. Any assistant (Claude, ChatGPT or another) continuing this project must read it first, follow it, and append new decisions here with a date. Do not reopen a "Decided" item without Ahmed asking.

Companion documents in this folder (repo README describes the app as built at R80):
- `HANDBOOK_R45.md` — how the app works as built at R45 (features, business rules, automations, open loops).
- `ROADMAP_GAP_REVIEW_R45.md` — the original roadmap checked against the code, and the critique of the R45 requirements document.
- `R46_BASELINE_HYGIENE_QA.md`, `R47_*` — per-iteration notes and two-phone test scripts.
- The live UX spec is a Claude Doc ("OneSpace UX Redesign Specification v1"); its content is summarised in section C below.

##### Current superseding decision — 20 Nov 2026

**FINAL, ACCEPTED: personal/private storage is tied permanently to each account; shared storage belongs to the specific current couple. One UI for both. No automated archiving, export, restore or My Archives is part of leaving, removal, shared-space deletion or account deletion.** When the relationship ends, permanently erase that pair's shared content after an explicit irreversible-loss warning, retain both persons' private records, and leave them unpaired. A new partner never sees an old relationship's shared records or anyone's private records. Account deletion has a **seven-day cancelable grace period**; at expiry delete the requesting account and private data, delete that pair's shared content, preserve the survivor's private content, and unpair the survivor.

**Authoritative decision record:** [ADR_2026-11-20_PERSONAL_SHARED_OWNERSHIP_AND_DELETION.md](ADR_2026-11-20_PERSONAL_SHARED_OWNERSHIP_AND_DELETION.md). This decision supersedes prior archive/restore proposals, old keep-shared-on-account-deletion behavior, and historical “prepare separation” workflows. No destructive QA or Production action may go live until it passes full preservation/privacy/security testing; old pending preparations do not constitute fresh irreversible consent.

##### A. Working agreement

| Rule | Detail |
|---|---|
| Repositories | ⚠️ OVERRIDDEN by D77 (17 Nov) and D92 (26 Nov 2026) — Same GitHub repo (BoyofKay/OneSpace), same Supabase project (OneSpace, ap-northeast-2), Firebase only for push. |
| Approval | ⚠️ OVERRIDDEN by D87 (26 Nov 2026) — Scope is agreed in writing before building. Ahmed approves merges to `main`. |
| Branches | ⚠️ OVERRIDDEN by D86 (26 Nov 2026) — One branch and one Pull Request per iteration (`rNN-short-name`). |
| Database | Every change = a numbered file in `supabase/schema/` AND a recorded Supabase migration. Non-destructive by default; never hard-delete user data. |
| Verification | ⚠️ OVERRIDDEN by D88 (26 Nov 2026) — GitHub workflow "Verify OneSpace iteration" (analyze + tests) must pass on the branch. |
| Release | A signed APK is built only when `release/READY_TO_BUILD.md` changes on `main`. Install as in-place update; never uninstall. |
| Testing | Every iteration ships a plain-language two-phone test script in `docs/`. |

##### B. Product decisions (dated)

| # | Date | Decision |
|---|---|---|
| D1 | 15 Nov 2026 | Automatic love rituals (morning/after-work/goodnight auto-sends) are RETIRED. Keep manual gestures and private "remind me to send a little love" self-reminders, to be made smart later. Done in R46. |
| D2 | 15 Nov 2026 | Shared Goals REMOVED from the app (table and data kept). Done in R46. |
| D3 | 15 Nov 2026 | Living arrangement = two facts: **How we live** (Together / Apart; a couple setting changeable anytime from the Today hero, saved instantly with Undo, partner gets a push; on Apart → Together the app offers to save "our journey apart" as a memory; nothing deleted, switching back restores counters) + **Where we are now** (automatic from each phone's time zone and active trips; clocks appear when zones differ; travel is detected). The app must serve ordinary couples, long-distance couples and a travelling partner. |
| D4 | 15 Nov 2026 | Partner is notified when shared items change. Push for meaningful changes (new item, date/time/place change, status change, assignment, @mention, message, removal/restore, gesture, letter, check-in); minor edits only in the Today catch-up feed. |
| D5 | 15 Nov 2026 | Quiet hours: alerts triggered by the partner wait until the recipient's wake-up time; the recipient's own reminders always fire on time; a partner may mark a reminder for you **Urgent**, which overrides sleep. Today is the catch-up place. |
| D6 | 15 Nov 2026 | Push notifications show content (partner name + action + item title/message), in the recipient's app language. Exceptions: Only-me items never reach the partner; nothing about a surprise is pushed to the honoree. |
| D7 | 15 Nov 2026 | Activity chat bubbles show initials only (already true in code). |
| D8 | 15 Nov 2026 | Shared memories: no mutual-delete workflow. Remove (restorable by either partner) + push to the other partner. |
| D9 | 15 Nov 2026 | Period tracking DROPPED. Medication times are covered by repeating reminders. |
| D10 | 15 Nov 2026 | Links between items: a link must save re-typing, carry a lifecycle, or show origin. Keep Activity option→Occasion gift, Activity contact→People, Trip→checklist, Flight→itinerary, Document→itinerary item, Reminder→source (fix: stop alerting when source closes). Simplify generic note linking into "Add a note" inside items. Add "Create from this" actions and one "Linked items" strip per item. |
| D11 | 15 Nov 2026 | Filters: slider (`tune`) icon, never a funnel. Opens a dropdown panel under the icon (not a bottom sheet) with groups of checkbox rows separated by lines, Clear + Apply pinned at the bottom, a dot on the icon and a "Showing: … ✕" line while active. Filters RESET when leaving a screen. |
| D12 | 15 Nov 2026 | History (clock icon) always opens a sub-page with a back arrow: who did what, when. A horizontal row of quick-filter chips: people (Everyone · Me · Partner) then actions (Created · Edited · Completed · Cancelled · Missed · Removed · Restored). Removed rows have a Restore button. |
| D13 | 15 Nov 2026 | Item ⋮ menu: final-status actions first, then Edit, Remove/Restore, Change log (= the history page filtered to that item). |
| D14 | 15 Nov 2026 | Status model: **Completed family** (Completed, Done, Watched, Purchased, Booked, Celebrated, Delivered) · **Cancelled** · **Missed** (system-set, only for recurring cycles that ended unfinished) · **Removed** (= delete; hidden, kept, restorable). One-time items past their date are **Overdue**, never Missed. The word "Archive" disappears from the app; the action is "Remove". Finished/Removed are filters, not part of the change log. |
| D15 | 15 Nov 2026 | Editor sub-page headers always read "Add new [type]" or "Edit [type]"; the item's name is an editable field, never the header. |
| D16 | 15 Nov 2026 | Creation flow: no create popups. "+" opens the item's full page in draft; purpose-based starters for workflow types (e.g. Activity: Buy something · Find a class or service · Plan an event · Decide something · Just a note); draft saves after a title, an empty draft is discarded (no half-empty records); in-place child creation; one contextual Quick add. |
| D17 | 15 Nov 2026 | Share-in: destinations suggested from WHAT is shared (shop link, media link, travel site, maps, unknown site, text with date, photo, PDF, contact) for any app or website. The tray is always the first choice. Tray name: **"Items to sort"** (not "Inbox", which clashes with letters). Placement: a shortcut tile with count, a Needs-attention row when items wait, an entry in the ⋮ menu; no permanent top-bar icon. Private by default. |
| D18 | 15 Nov 2026 | Today: five sections, fixed order: Hero · Since you were away · Needs your attention · Today and the next 7 days · Our space shortcuts. Empty sections show a kind hint ("You're all caught up", "Nothing is waiting on you right now"), never disappear. "A little closer" is a shortcut tile, not a section. Greeting: "Good evening, Ahmed" then "Your space with your [preferred label]". |
| D19 | 15 Nov 2026 | Shortcuts cover ALL features: 3-column grid grouped by tab — Remember: Calendar, To-do & Checklists, Reminders, Notes, Occasions & surprises, People · Coordinate: Activities · Connect: Letters, A little love, Check-ins, Shared experiences, Memories · World: Trips & journeys · Tools: Items to sort, Search our space. Count badge only when action is needed. |
| D20 | 15 Nov 2026 | Push batching for chat messages: at most 1 push per item per 10 minutes ("+3 more"); revisit after real use. |
| D21 | 15 Nov 2026 | ⚠️ OVERRIDDEN by D83 (20 Nov 2026) — Brand line: **"Two of us, One Space"** (English in both languages), shown under the OneSpace name in the top bar on every main screen, on the splash and on sign-in. Replaced the first version "As a couple, we have One Space" at Ahmed's request. |
| D22 | 15 Nov 2026 | Innovation Lab Session 01 (Today) verdict accepted: keep Awareness/Connection/Action, Agenda vs Attention split, "good time to talk" windows computed ONLY from stated schedules, local-first cached Today (≤0.5 s from saved copy, ≤2 s fresh), notifications need reason/owner/expiry/action, no half-empty records from quick actions, hideable "on this day" memories. Rejected: hidden push previews, private-by-default, invented approval/vote/confirmation items, incomplete-profile nags, manual Busy status, partner confirmation for arrangement changes, renaming tabs, scores of any kind. Default sharing = Shared, with Only-me exceptions. Each Lab session ends with decisions + backlog with acceptance criteria + won't-do list. |
| D23 | 15 Nov 2026 | R48 push rules (my design within D5/D6/D20, open to change after use): several quick changes to the same item by the partner within **2 minutes** share one push; a notification already opened in the app is not pushed; alerts held by quiet hours arrive as **one wake-up summary** per phone; a newer push about the same item replaces the older one in the tray; push wording uses the name the recipient calls their partner (Profile partner label), else the partner's first name. Minor edits (wording, notes, tags) never push. |
| D24 | 15 Nov 2026 | R49 Today: one server call (`onespace_today`) with your own access rules; a saved copy on the phone shows first, then refreshes ("Last updated · Retry" when offline). Greeting uses an endearment as "your love", a plain label as "Kay". Travelling comes only from a shared trip happening today (no location tracking).
